are unchanged from 019fc60: ITL about -8% from fewer verify rows per
request, ITL failures near zero, throughput back to the prefill bound,
score about 7000; outputs are unchanged because verification is greedy
against the target model. Ruff, shell syntax and the full test suite
(348 tests) pass on the merged tree.
